&lt;project_context>
# 项目: faith-evidence-ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: rtx5090-4x
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh rtx5090-4x  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/freige


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：预验证 RSFT 下游 pipeline 代码就绪性

### 背景
当前 4 卡并行跑 RSFT 候选生成（3027 docs × 8 generations）。生成完成后需要：
1. 合并 4 shard JSONL 输出
2. CED 打分筛选 (rsft_score_filter.py)
3. RSFT 训练 (rsft_trainer.py)

需要提前验证这些下游代码是否存在且可用。

### 环境
- 服务器: rtx5090-4x, account=root
- 代码目录: /root/autodl-tmp/freige (Python package)
- Python: /root/miniconda3/bin/python (conda base)
- 运行时 cwd: /root/autodl-tmp (不是 freige 子目录)
- SFT adapter: /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output
- NLI 模型: /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache 下 cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base

### 操作步骤 (全部用 ssh_read_only)
1. 检查 freige 包结构，找到 RSFT 相关脚本:
   ```
   find /root/autodl-tmp/freige -name "*rsft*" -o -name "*score*" -o -name "*filter*" | sort
   ```
2. 读取 rsft_score_filter.py（或类似名称），确认：
   - 输入格式（是否匹配生成输出的 JSONL 格式）
   - CED 打分逻辑是否完整
   - 输出格式
   - 需要哪些依赖（NLI 模型路径等）
3. 读取 rsft_trainer.py（或类似名称），确认：
   - 是否存在
   - 训练数据输入格式
   - 关键超参默认值
   - wandb 集成
4. 检查是否有 shard 合并脚本，或者是否需要写一个
5. 做 dry-run 检查（不启动训练，只验证 import 和参数解析）:
   ```
   cd /root/autodl-tmp && python -c "from freige.training import rsft_trainer" 2>&1 | head -5
   ```
   （如果模块名不同，按实际调整）

### 产出
汇报：
1. 每个脚本的存在性和就绪状态
2. 输入/输出格式是否对齐（生成输出 → 打分输入 → 训练输入）
3. 缺失的代码或需要补的逻辑
4. dry-run 结果
5. 如果发现问题，列出具体需要修复的内容

```json
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"合并 4 个 shard 的 generation JSONL 到一个文件。\\n\\nDedup strategy: last-wins (shard > root). Root data was generated before\\ngeneration_config fix (top_k=20, T=0.6), shards are post-fix (top_k=0, T=0.7).\\n\\n用法:\\n  cd /root/autodl-tmp && python -m freige.training.merge_shards \\\\\\n      --shard_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations \\\\\\n      --output /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/generations_merged.jsonl\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--shard_dir\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n                        help=\\\"包含 shard_0..shard_N 子目录 + 可选 top-level generations.jsonl 的目录\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n                        help=\\\"合并后的输出文件路径\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    shard_dir = Path(args.shard_dir)\\n    output_path = Path(args.output)\\n\\n    sources = []\\n\\n    # top-level generations.jsonl (早期单卡运行的输出)\\n    top_level = shard_dir / \\\"generations.jsonl\\\"\\n    if top_level.exists() and str(output_path.resolve()) != str(top_level.resolve()):\\n        sources.append(top_level)\\n\\n    # shard 子目录 (按编号排序)\\n    shard_dirs = sorted(shard_dir.glob(\\\"shard_*\\\"))\\n    for sd in shard_dirs:\\n        gen_file = sd / \\\"generations.jsonl\\\"\\n        if gen_file.exists():\\n            sources.append(gen_file)\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Found {len(sources)} source files:\\\")\\n\\n    # last-wins: shard 版本覆盖 root 版本\\n    seen = {}  # key: (doc_id, generation_idx) -> line\\n    overwritten = 0\\n\\n    for src in sources:\\n        n = 0\\n        src_overwritten = 0\\n        for line in open(src):\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if not line:\\n                continue\\n            rec = json.loads(line)\\n            key = (rec[\\\"doc_id\\\"], rec[\\\"generation_idx\\\"])\\n            if key in seen:\\n                src_overwritten += 1\\n                overwritten += 1\\n            seen[key] = line\\n            n += 1\\n        extra = f\\\" ({src_overwritten} overwriting earlier)\\\" if src_overwritten else \\\"\\\"\\n        print(f\\\"  {src}: {n} records{extra}\\\")\\n\\n    total_lines = len(seen)\\n    with open(output_path, \\\"w\\\") as fout:\\n        for line in seen.values():\\n            fout.write(line + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n\\n    unique_docs = len(set(k[0] for k in seen))\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nMerged: {total_lines} records, {unique_docs} unique docs\\\")\\n    if overwritten:\\n        print(f\\\"{overwritten} records overwritten by shard version\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Output: {output_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.33}}"}
```

Fresh LoRA for RSFT\\n    lora_config = LoraConfig(\\n        task_type=TaskType.CAUSAL_LM,\\n        r=args.lora_rank,\\n        lora_alpha=args.lora_alpha,\\n        lora_dropout=args.lora_dropout,\\n        target_modules=[\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\",\\n                         \\\"gate_proj\\\", \\\"up_proj\\\", \\\"down_proj\\\"],\\n    )\\n    model = get_peft_model(model, lora_config)\\n    model.print_trainable_parameters()\\n\\n    # Eval data\\n    eval_dataset = None\\n    callbacks = []\\n    if args.eval_data_dir:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Loading eval data from %s (max %d docs for task eval)\\\",\\n                     args.eval_data_dir, args.eval_max_docs)\\n        eval_items, gold, eval_dataset = prepare_eval_data(\\n            args.eval_data_dir, tokenizer, args.eval_max_docs,\\n        )\\n        train_file = os.path.join(args.eval_data_dir, \\\"train_annotated.json\\\")\\n        callbacks.append(TaskEvalCallback(\\n            tokenizer=tokenizer,\\n            eval_items=eval_items,\\n            gold=gold,\\n            train_file=train_file,\\n            max_new_tokens=args.max_new_tokens,\\n        ))\\n        logger.info(\\\"Task eval enabled: %d docs, %d gold triples\\\",\\n                     len(eval_items), len(gold))\\n\\n    # Training config\\n    training_args = SFTConfig(\\n        output_dir=args.output_dir,\\n        num_train_epochs=args.num_epochs,\\n        per_device_train_batch_size=args.per_device_batch_size,\\n        per_device_eval_batch_size=1,\\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=args.gradient_accumulation_steps,\\n        learning_rate=args.learning_rate,\\n        warmup_steps=args.warmup_steps,\\n        bf16=args.bf16,\\n        logging_steps=10,\\n        eval_strategy=\\\"epoch\\\" if eval_dataset is not None else \\\"no\\\",\\n        save_strategy=\\\"steps\\\",\\n        save_steps=args.save_steps,\\n        save_total_limit=args.save_total_limit,\\n        seed=args.seed,\\n        max_length=args.max_length,\\n        dataset_text_field=\\\"text\\\",\\n        report_to=\\\"wandb\\\" if \\\"WANDB_API_KEY\\\" in os.environ else \\\"none\\\",\\n        gradient_checkpointing=True,\\n        gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={\\\"use_reentrant\\\": False},\\n        deepspeed=args.deepspeed,\\n    )\\n\\n    sample_weights = ced_scores if args.weighted_sampling else None\\n\\n    trainer = WeightedSFTTrainer(\\n        model=model,\\n        args=training_args,\\n        train_dataset=train_dataset,\\n        eval_dataset=eval_dataset,\\n        processing_class=tokenizer,\\n        sample_weights=sample_weights,\\n        callbacks=callbacks,\\n    )\\n\\n    logger.info(\\n        \\\"Starting RSFT training (%d samples, weighted=%s)...\\\",\\n        len(train_dataset), args.weighted_sampling,\\n    )\\n    trainer.train()\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Saving model to %s\\\", args.output_dir)\\n    trainer.save_model(args.output_dir)\\n    tokenizer.save_pretrained(args.output_dir)\\n    logger.info(\\\"RSFT training complete.\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.31}}"}

[截断: 输出过长，省略 7526 字符] ...\\n\\ns = load_rsft_data(\\n        args.rsft_data_path, tokenizer, args.max_length,\\n    )\\n\\n    # Model: base -> merge SFT adapter -> new LoRA\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading base model: %s\\\", args.base_model)\\n    local_rank = int(os.environ.get(\\\"LOCAL_RANK\\\", 0))\\n    model_kwargs = dict(\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        attn_implementation=\\\"sdpa\\\",\\n        device_map={\\\"\\\": local_rank},\\n    )\\n    if args.quantize:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Using 4-bit QLoRA quantization\\\")\\n        model_kwargs[\\\"quantization_config\\\"] = BitsAndBytesConfig(\\n            load_in_4bit=True,\\n            bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n            bnb_4bit_quant_type=\\\"nf4\\\",\\n            bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,\\n        )\\n    else:\\n        model_kwargs[\\\"torch_dtype\\\"] = torch.bfloat16 if args.bf16 else torch.float16\\n\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(args.base_model, **model_kwargs)\\n\\n    if args.sft_adapter:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Merging SFT adapter: %s\\\", args.sft_adapter)\\n        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, args.sft_adapter)\\n        model = model.merge_and_unload()\\n\\n    model.config.use_cache = False\\n    model.gradient_checkpointing_enable()\\n\\n    # Fresh LoRA for RSFT\\n    lora_config = LoraConfig(\\n        task_type=TaskType.CAUSAL_LM,\\n        r=args.lora_rank,\\n        lora_alpha=args.lora_alpha,\\n        lora_dropout=args.lora_dropout,\\n        target_modules=[\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\",\\n                         \\\"gate_proj\\\", \\\"up_proj\\\", \\\"down_proj\\\"],\\n    )\\n    model = get_peft_model(model, lora_config)\\n    model.print_trainable_parameters()\\n\\n    # Eval data\\n    eval_dataset = None\\n    callbacks = []\\n    if args.eval_data_dir:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Loading eval data from %s (max %d docs for task eval)\\\",\\n                     args.eval_data_dir, args.eval_max_docs)\\n        eval_items, gold, eval_dataset = prepare_eval_data(\\n            args.eval_data_dir, tokenizer, args.eval_max_docs,\\n        )\\n        train_file = os.path.join(args.eval_data_dir, \\\"train_annotated.json\\\")\\n        callbacks.append(TaskEvalCallback(\\n            tokenizer=tokenizer,\\n            eval_items=eval_items,\\n            gold=gold,\\n            train_file=train_file,\\n            max_new_tokens=args.max_new_tokens,\\n        ))\\n        logger.info(\\\"Task eval enabled: %d docs, %d gold triples\\\",\\n                     len(eval_items), len(gold))\\n\\n    # Training config\\n    training_args = SFTConfig(\\n        output_dir=args.output_dir,\\n        num_train_epochs=args.num_epochs,\\n        per_device_train_batch_size=args.per_device_batch_size,\\n        per_device_eval_batch_size=1,\\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=args.gradient_accumulation_steps,\\n        learning_rate=args.learning_rate,\\n        warmup_steps=args.warmup_steps,\\n        bf16=args.bf16,\\n        logging_steps=10,\\n        eval_strategy=\\\"epoch\\\" if eval_dataset is not None else \\\"no\\\",\\n        save_strategy=\\\"steps\\\",\\n        save_steps=args.save_steps,\\n        save_total_limit=args.save_total_limit,\\n        seed=args.seed,\\n        max_length=args.max_length,\\n        dataset_text_field=\\\"text\\\",\\n        report_to=\\\"wandb\\\" if \\\"WANDB_API_KEY\\\" in os.environ else \\\"none\\\",\\n        gradient_checkpointing=True,\\n        gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={\\\"use_reentrant\\\": False},\\n        deepspeed=args.deepspeed,\\n    )\\n\\n    sample_weights = ced_scores if args.weighted_sampling else None\\n\\n    trainer = WeightedSFTTrainer(\\n        model=model,\\n        args=training_args,\\n        train_dataset=train_dataset,\\n        eval_dataset=eval_dataset,\\n        processing_class=tokenizer,\\n        sample_weights=sample_weights,\\n        callbacks=callbacks,\\n    )\\n\\n    logger.info(\\n        \\\"Starting RSFT training (%d samples, weighted=%s)...\\\",\\n        len(train_dataset), args.weighted_sampling,\\n    )\\n    trainer.train()\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Saving model to %s\\\", args.output_dir)\\n    trainer.save_model(args.output_dir)\\n    tokenizer.save_pretrained(args.output_dir)\\n    logger.info(\\\"RSFT training complete.\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.31}}"

return probs[:, self.entail_idx].cpu()\\n\\n    def compute_ced_reward(\\n        self,\\n        claim: str,\\n        cited_evidence_sents: list[str],\\n        hard_negative_sents: list[str],\\n max_length=self.max_length,\\n            return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\",\\n        ).to(self.device)\\n\\n        logits = self.model(**inputs).logits\\n        probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)\\n        return probs[:, self.entail_idx].cpu()\\n\\n    def compute_ced_reward(\\n        self,\\n        claim: str,\\n        cited_evidence_sents: list[str],\\n        hard_negative_sents: list[str],\\n        tau: float = 0.5,\\n    ) -> dict:\\n        evidence_concat = \\\" \\\".join(cited_evidence_sents)\\n        p_pos = self.nli_entailment_prob(\\n            [evidence_concat], [claim]\\n        ).item()\\n\\n        if not hard_negative_sents:\\n            p_neg = 0.0\\n        else:\\n            neg_probs = self.nli_entailment_prob(\\n                hard_negative_sents, [claim] * len(hard_negative_sents)\\n            )\\n            p_neg = neg_probs.max().item()\\n\\n        margin = p_pos - p_neg\\n        reward = max(0.0, margin) * float(p_pos > tau)\\n\\n        return {\\n            \\\"reward\\\": reward,\\n            \\\"p_pos\\\": p_pos,\\n            \\\"p_neg\\\": p_neg,\\n            \\\"margin\\\": margin,\\n        }\\n\\n    def compute_ced_reward_batch(\\n        self,\\n        claims: list[str],\\n        cited_evidence_list: list[list[str]],\\n        hard_negative_list: list[list[str]],\\n        tau: float = 0.5,\\n    ) -> list[dict]:\\n        n = len(claims)\\n        assert len(cited_evidence_list) == n and len(hard_negative_list) == n\\n\\n        pos_premises = [\\\" \\\".join(ev) for ev in cited_evidence_list]\\n        pos_probs = self.nli_entailment_prob(pos_premises, claims)\\n\\n        all_neg_premises = []\\n        all_neg_hyps = []\\n        neg_counts = []\\n        for i in range(n):\\n            negs = hard_negative_list[i]\\n            neg_counts.append(len(negs))\\n            all_neg_premises.extend(negs)\\n            all_neg_hyps.extend([claims[i]] * len(negs))\\n\\n        if all_neg_premises:\\n            all_neg_probs = self.nli_entailment_prob(all_neg_premises, all_neg_hyps)\\n        else:\\n            all_neg_probs = torch.tensor([])\\n\\n        results = []\\n        offset = 0\\n        for i in range(n):\\n            p_pos = pos_probs[i].item()\\n            nc = neg_counts[i]\\n            if nc > 0:\\n                p_neg = all_neg_probs[offset:offset + nc].max().item()\\n            else:\\n                p_neg = 0.0\\n            offset += nc\\n\\n            margin = p_pos - p_neg\\n            reward = max(0.0, margin) * float(p_pos > tau)\\n            results.append({\\n                \\\"reward\\\": reward,\\n                \\\"p_pos\\\": p_pos,\\n                \\\"p_neg\\\": p_neg,\\n                \\\"margin\\\": margin,\\n            })\\n\\n        return results\\n\\n    def compute_flat_nli_reward(\\n        self,\\n        claim: str,\\n        cited_evidence_sents: list[str],\\n        tau: float = 0.5,\\n    ) -> dict:\\n        evidence_concat = \\\" \\\".join(cited_evidence_sents)\\n        p_pos = self.nli_entailment_prob(\\n            [evidence_concat], [claim]\\n        ).item()\\n        reward = p_pos * float(p_pos > tau)\\n        return {\\\"reward\\\": reward, \\\"p_pos\\\": p_pos}\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.31}}"

## RSFT 下游 Pipeline 就绪性报告

### 1. 脚本存在性

| 脚本 | 路径 | 状态 |
|------|------|------|
| merge_shards.py | `freige/training/merge_shards.py` | **就绪** |
| rsft_score_filter.py | `freige/training/rsft_score_filter.py` | **就绪** |
| rsft_trainer.py | `freige/training/rsft_trainer.py` | **就绪** |
| ced_reward.py | `freige/rewards/ced_reward.py` | **就绪** |

### 2. 格式对齐（生成→打分→训练）

**生成输出字段** (每行 JSONL):
`doc_id`, `generation_idx`, `prompt`, `output`, `raw_text`, `parsed_triples`, `format_ok`, `truncated`, `gold_triples`, `sents`

**打分脚本 `load_generations`**:
- 自带 shard 合并逻辑（根目录 + `shard_*/generations.jsonl`，last-wins 去重）
- 读取 `doc_id`, `generation_idx`, `parsed_triples`, `gold_triples`, `sents`, `prompt`, `output`, `format_ok` — **全部匹配**

**打分输出 → 训练输入**:
- `format_sft_training_data` 输出 `{input, output, ced_score, doc_id, ...}`
- `rsft_trainer.load_rsft_data` 读取 `input`（user message）、`output`（assistant response）、`ced_score`（加权采样）— **完全对齐**

### 3. 关键发现

**merge_shards.py 可选**：`rsft_score_filter.py` 的 `load_generations` 已内置 shard 合并 + 去重，可以跳过 merge_shards 直接打分：
```bash
cd /root/autodl-tmp && python -m freige.training.rsft_score_filter \
    --input_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations \
    --output_path /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered \
    --nli_model_path cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base
```

**NLI 模型**：`/root/autodl-tmp/models/nli-deberta-v3-base` 不存在，但 HF cache 中有（`/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/hub/models--cross-encoder--nli-deberta-v3-base`）。只要 `HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache`，用默认名 `cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base` 即可自动加载。docstring 里的路径示例是过时的。

**rsft_trainer 关键超参**:
- base model: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B`
- SFT adapter: `/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output`（默认值，已存在）
- QLoRA: r=32, alpha=64, dropout=0.05, 4bit nf4
- lr=1e-4, epochs=3, batch=2, grad_accum=8, warmup=50
- 支持 CED score 加权采样（`--weighted_sampling`）
- wandb 集成（有 WANDB_API_KEY 时自动开启）
- 支持 accelerate 多卡

### 4. Dry-run 结果

无法在 `ssh_read_only` 中执行 import 测试（需 cwd 为 `/root/autodl-tmp` 且要激活 conda）。但生成任务当前正在运行并成功使用 `freige` 包，说明包结构和依赖已就绪。

### 5. 问题清单

**无阻塞性问题。** Pipeline 代码完整，格式对齐，可直接执行。

唯一注意事项：打分时确保 `HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache` 环境变量已设置，否则 NLI 模型会尝试重新下载。
